# Skills · A · part 220

881 entries, arena to argocd-gitops-adoption. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **arena**: Compare independent drafts and combine their strongest ideas when an artifact's structure is still unsettled. ([greenfield-inc/agent-farm](https://github.com/greenfield-inc/agent-farm/tree/HEAD/plugins/orchestra/skills/arena) · ★9)
- **arena**: Spawn N parallel candidates at the same task, pick a base, graft the strongest parts of the losers into it. Use for 'arena this', 'throw it in the arena', or when one attempt at a non-trivial artifact would lock in the wrong shape. This is the detailed procedure for the team-patterns judge panel. ([taljeon/plzdo](https://github.com/taljeon/plzdo/tree/HEAD/skills/arena) · ★9)
- **arena**: Make many versions of the agent compete over one task. Spins up N sub-agents (default 16, --agents 100 for a full arena), gives every one the exact same task plus a different strategy card (reasoning mode, workflow, strategy), then runs a single-elimination bracket: they attack each other's solutions, defend and revise, and a judge scores every match on a written rubric until one solution survives. Use when the user is not satisfied with an answer, calls it a bad answer, says try again or do better, says "arena", or asks to make them compete. ([0v3rf3ar/Eirene](https://github.com/0v3rf3ar/Eirene/tree/HEAD/eirene/plugins/arena/skills/arena) · ★6)

Use for /arena, "arena this", "throw it in the arena", or when a single attempt at a non-trivial artifact would lock in the wrong shape. ([prdai/dotfiles](https://github.com/prdai/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/arena) · ★6)

- **arfiti-account-choice**: Choose the ledger account for one purchase in Arfiti - a card charge the bank agent posts or a line of a supplier bill - from the records, in a fixed order of authority (client rule, vendor default, vendor history, vendor notes, the chart of accounts), and say which evidence decided it. Use whenever a posting needs an expense, cost or asset account that the caller's skill does not already fix. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/tree/HEAD/arfiti-core-agent-procedures/skills/arfiti-account-choice) · ★5)
- **arfiti-bank-statement-lines**: Settle one bank account's (or card programme's) unreconciled statement lines in Arfiti through the MCP tools - confirm a payment-batch line, post a bank entry or payment through submit anchored to the statement line and match it, or exclude a duplicate - in a fixed order of checks, never breaking a tie with a date or an amount, and reading the line back after every write. Use for every bank session. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/tree/HEAD/arfiti-core-agent-procedures/skills/arfiti-bank-statement-lines) · ★5)
- **arfiti-bill-processing**: Process one incoming vendor bill in Arfiti end to end through the MCP tools only - read the document, find or delegate the vendor, choose accounts and tax codes from vendor defaults, history and the organisation's rules, check for a duplicate, validate, post through submit, attach the source document, and report. Use for every bill session. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/tree/HEAD/arfiti-core-agent-procedures/skills/arfiti-bill-processing) · ★5)
- **arfiti-billing-usage-answers**: Turn a person's answer to a "provide usage data" task into recorded contract usage and a billing run for exactly those contracts, through the Arfiti MCP tools - read the task and the answer, map each figure to a metered contract line, record it with submit("contract_usage", "record"), then submit("billing_run", "execute") for those contracts. Never invents a quantity. Use when a usage-data task has been answered. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/tree/HEAD/arfiti-core-agent-procedures/skills/arfiti-billing-usage-answers) · ★5)
- **arfiti-configuration**: Resolve one accounting-configuration problem in Arfiti through the MCP tools - accounts, posting profiles, transaction-type posting links, fiscal periods, payment terms, tax codes, a confirmed entity time zone - by finding what exists, choosing the smallest change that only affects future postings, validating, submitting, and reading back. Refuses on its own to change anything that would reclassify or re-report posted history. Use for every configuration task, whether a session's own or a brief from another agent.
